6. Ruling inbox. A Telegram message from the owner's chat that starts with "→ BUILD" is archived verbatim to docs/dispatches/inbox/<time>.md and becomes the next queue item; the build session's continue mechanism (R-AUTO-CONTINUE) takes inbox items in order. A message starting with "→ AUDIT" triggers a fresh independent audit run exactly the way the supervisor runs (docs/AUDITOR-TEMPLATE.md, fresh model, reads only repo + mirror files), whose report lands on Reports and as one Telegram line. The interactive audit session is retired. Bot replies "queued: <first words>" so the owner knows it landed. Owner chat id only.

"""PART 9 (E1-FULL-1): ring the laptop when a Claude Code session finishes, and send one [CODE]
line through the existing Telegram bot so the owner comes back. Wired as a Stop hook in
.claude/settings.json. Sound first, message second — the sound is what wakes the room.
"""
import os
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))


def ring():
    """R-E1-SIREN-WINDOW: ring the session-finished event through the VISIBLE always-on-top alarm window
    (never headless sound). Best-effort; never fatal to the session."""
    try:
        from scripts import ring as ring_window
        return ring_window.ring("✅ Claude Code session finished — come back")
    except Exception:
        return False


def main():
    ring()                        # open the stoppable alarm window
    try:
        from scripts.notify import notify
        notify("[CODE] session finished — come back")
    except Exception:
        pass                      # a missing network must never hold the session open


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

tool_use Bash {"command": "sed -n \"$(grep -n 'def _with_link' scripts/notify.py | head -1 | cut -d: -f1),+8p\" scripts/notify.py", "description": "Read notify _with_link"}
tool_result: def _with_link(message: str) -> str:
    """R-E1-ALERT-OWN + R-E1-THREE-EMOJIS: a summons (🙋), a completion (✅), or a session-finish ([CODE])
    line carries a tap-to-open link to the live site (which MacroDroid can open). The ⏱ progress lines and
    other info lines are untouched, and a message that already has the link is not doubled."""
    m = str(message)
    is_alert = ("\U0001f64b" in m) or ("✅" in m) or m.lstrip().startswith("[CODE]")
    if is_alert and _SITE not in m:
        return m.rstrip() + " — open " + _SITE
    return m

tool_result: def build(write=True, only=None):
    """Port the pages we have providers for, overwriting the old site/v2/ pages. Returns {served: problems}.
    A page with gate problems is NOT written (the last-good page stays)."""
    os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
    results = {}
    for dc_name, provider in PROVIDERS.items():
        if only and dc_name not in only:
            continue
        served = HREF[dc_name]
        html = port(dc_name, provider())
        probs = _gate(html)
        results[served] = probs
        if write and not probs:
            with open(os.path.join(OUT, served), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(html)
    return results

=== script renderVals ===
<script type="text/x-dc" data-dc-script data-props=’{"$preview":{"width":1280,"height":1820}}’>
class Component extends DCLogic {
constructor(props) { super(props); this.state = { expanded: false }; }
renderVals() {
const e = this.state.expanded;
return { expanded: e, cols: e ? '2fr 1fr 0.8fr 0.8fr 0.8fr 0.7fr 0.9fr 0.9fr 0.9fr 0.7fr 0.7fr 0.8fr 1.1fr' : '2fr 1fr 0.8fr 0.8fr 0.8fr 0.7fr 0.9fr 0.9fr 0.9fr 0.7fr', expandLabel: e ? 'Fewer columns' : 'Expand - fees, hold time, exit rule', toggle: () => this.setState({ expanded: !e }) };
}
}
</script>
